10 oUSTeS:GAWrE. [OCT.-DBC., CUS$TOM HOUSE, NEWOHWANG, 23RD DEcEmBRR 1902. SIR, L.-The Imperial Russian Provisional Civil Administration having opened, on the 12th August 19oo, a Custom House to colleet, under Foreign supervision, Dues and Likin on Junk Cargoes, I now transmit the statistias of that -trade from i st October to 3 1st December 19o2.. On all cargoes arriving oi leaving by sea-going junks Dues and Duties were collected at Treaty Tariff rates and Likin at the rate of A per cent. ad alorem on Imports and A per cent. ad valorem on Exports, with the exception of the Duties on certain specified staples, which were reduced on ist May 1901 to the rate formerly assessed by the Native Customs. Boats trading up river paid Tonnage Dues, but no Duties were levied on their cargoes. II. 236 CUSTOMS GAZETTE. OCT.-DEC.2 (Received 16th JanUary T903.) CUSTOM HOUSE, KOWLOON, gr JAvAR 1903. SIR, I.-a. In conformity with the instructions contained in your Circular No. 381, Second Series, I now transmit to the Statistical Department at Shanghai, for publication, the statistics of the trade at Kowloon and the neighbouring stations in Native vessels for the fourth quarter of the year 1902. * b. The stations at present controlled by the Kowloon Customs are Taishan (* g), Lintin ( 1), and Shamchun (n jJ1J), on the north and west; Samun (= fl), on the east; and Shaiichung ($ gf3) and Shatowkok ( A ), on the Mirs Bay littoral. The Custom House is called ng &pound; E. c.The word &quot; Inwards&quot; in these statistics means to China, and the word &quot; Outwards,&quot; from China. II.
